- **2026-09-25 — CLAUDE — GET /api/agent-model-mix production BigInt crash fix (branch `claude/agent-model-mix-fix`).**  Item 3/4 of the Sentry agent-telemetry plan (weekly fleet-mode compliance digest) -- the endpoint PR #1539 shipped 500ed on every production call (masked as an empty result by its own fail-closed catch); PR #1541 temporarily surfaced the real error, which turned out to be `RangeError: The number 0.3498095 cannot be converted to a BigInt because it is not an integer`.  Root cause: `SUM(CASE ...)` aggregates with no explicit cast let SQLite's per-row storage-class typing lock Prisma's `$queryRaw` column inference to BigInt when the first GROUP BY bucket's sum happened to be an exact integer, then crash on a later group's real fractional dollar amount.  Fixed by wrapping both SUMs in `CAST(... AS REAL)`; reproduced the exact crash against a real SQLite db before the fix and confirmed it gone after (new `agent-model-mix.db.test.ts`).  Also removed the temporary diagnostic that leaked the raw error message + stack trace to any caller with a read token -- restored the standard fail-closed contract now that the underlying bug is fixed.  Doc: `docs/rollouts/2026-09-25-agent-model-mix-bigint-fix.md`.  **Merged and deployed** as PR #1546 (merge commit `0e29da0d`, merged directly by the owner while CI was green); live-verified `GET /api/agent-model-mix?days=7` returning `200` with real fleet data post-deploy; board 52452cb2e406492a8b240977acdeef23.
- **2026-09-25 — CLAUDE — GET /api/agent-model-mix NULL-first BigInt variant + hardening (branch `claude/agent-model-mix-followup`).**  Follow-up to the row above, from PR #1546's own adversarial review (which finished after the owner had already merged it, so this is a new PR rather than an amend).  Found and fixed a second BigInt-crash trigger in the same aggregate: `COALESCE(CAST(SUM(...) AS REAL), 0)` still returned the untyped literal `0` (re-locking Prisma's `$queryRaw` column inference to BigInt) whenever the FIRST `GROUP BY` bucket's matching rows were all NULL rather than an exact integer.  Fixed by switching both `agent-model-mix.ts` and the identically-shaped sibling aggregate in `cost-by-session.ts` from `COALESCE(SUM(...), 0)`/`COALESCE(CAST(SUM(...) AS REAL), 0)` to plain `TOTAL(...)`, which always returns a REAL and never NULL.  Reproduced the exact `RangeError` against real SQLite for all three new NULL-first regression cases before the fix, confirmed gone after.  Also added `console.warn` logging to `loadAgentModelMixRows`'s fail-closed catch (previously silent, the same class of gap that hid the original incident), corrected a false "same contract as `GET /api/cost-by-session`" claim in `route.ts`'s comment, and fixed this row's stale "not merged" status.  Doc: `docs/rollouts/2026-09-25-agent-model-mix-null-first-fix.md`.  Board 52452cb2e406492a8b240977acdeef23.

Removed the Associated Domains entitlement (unowned domain) and the unused App Group on the iOS Safari extension; nulled the old ASC Apple IDs in `apps.json` (`ascRecordPending`) with ship + listing scripts refusing until new records exist; removed `LocalDataMigration` (Local data and provider keys start fresh); corrected the App ID + capability list.  Details: `docs/rollouts/2026-09-23-usage-identifier-fixes.md`.

Companion Safari renames: macOS Safari host app `services.jays.usage.monitor.safari` → `com.simplewithus.usagemonitor.macos.safari`, macOS Safari extension `…Extension` → `com.simplewithus.usagemonitor.safari.macos`, iOS Safari host app `…safari` → `com.simplewithus.usagemonitor.ios.safari`, iOS Safari extension `…Extension` → `com.simplewithus.usagemonitor.safari.ios`. - **2026-08-31 — CLAUDE — COMPLETED/MERGED #1386 `8ca36aff` — Three collectors had a main guard that silently no-ops on paths with spaces (branch `claude/collector-main-guard-hardening`, worktree `~/apps/usage-claude-ci-drift`).**  #1383 said its bare-filename guard was the only bad one in `scripts/`; true as written, but an audit of all four entrypoint idioms found three more. `claude-usage-collector.mjs`, `fleet-usage-collector.mjs`, and `antigravity-session-collector.mjs` used `import.meta.url.endsWith(process.argv[1])` -- `import.meta.url` percent-encodes the path and `argv[1]` does not, so on any checkout whose path contains a space (this fleet has them: `~/Code/Agentic Trading`) the guard is false, the CLI body is skipped, nothing is collected, and the process **exits 0**.  A LaunchAgent would report success forever while telemetry stopped arriving, and `com.jays.fleet-usage-collector.plist.example` exists to schedule exactly that.  Opposite direction to #1383 (too-strict vs too-loose), same root cause: comparing path fragments instead of resolved URLs.  Proven end-to-end from a spaced-path copy -- zero output before, collector runs after.  Switched all three to the `pathToFileURL` equality idiom the other seven scripts use, plus a repo-wide guard audit added to `test-session-token-collectors.mjs` (already a CI gate since #1381, so no new CI time), with a negative test proving it catches a reintroduction.  Latent, not currently firing: the four launchd-registered collectors already use the correct idiom.  Rollout: `docs/rollouts/2026-08-31-collector-main-guard-hardening.md`.

- **2026-08-31 — CLAUDE — COMPLETED/MERGED #1385 — Five workflows documented a `UM_CI_RUNNER` runner-offload feature that does not exist (branch `claude/stale-um-ci-runner-comments`, worktree `~/apps/usage-claude-ci-drift`).**  Found while wiring #1381.  `bbf540a0` (#834, 2026-07-29, owner-authored) replaced the dormant `${{ (github.actor != 'dependabot[bot]' && vars.UM_CI_RUNNER) || 'ubuntu-latest' }}` gate with a literal `ubuntu-latest` in all five workflows and left every explanatory comment behind, so for a month `ci.yml`, `security.yml`, `codeql.yml`, `uptime-monitor.yml`, and `effort-issues-sync.yml` described a live feature that had been deleted.  `vars.UM_CI_RUNNER` is read nowhere; the documented emergency runbook (`gh variable set UM_CI_RUNNER --body ""`) is inert, and the comments actively invite reintroducing the retired self-hosted runners (`codeql.yml` says to "revert this line" -- a line that no longer exists).  Comments and docs only; **no `runs-on:` value touched**, CI routing byte-identical to main.  Durable fix in `AGENTS.md`: every `verify` gate needs a matching `ci.yml` step in the same PR (+ audit one-liner), fleet CI is GitHub-hosted only, and trust the `runs-on:` value over any comment describing runner routing.  Rollout: `docs/rollouts/2026-08-31-stale-um-ci-runner-comments.md`.

- **2026-08-31 — CLAUDE — COMPLETED/MERGED #1383 `32bb1232` — CI verify-job drift #4: `test:r2-archive`, and the main-guard bug that hid it (branch `claude/ci-verify-r2-archive`, worktree `~/apps/usage-claude-r2-ci`, board `ff671b6e`).**  Closes the drift class #1381 opened: `test:r2-archive` was the fifteenth `npm run verify` gate with no `ci.yml` step, leaving the weekly R2 disaster-recovery archive (SigV4 signer, prune allowlist, verify-before-delete ordering, failure classifier) with zero CI coverage.  It could not just be wired: on clean `origin/main` the script printed `18 passed, 0 failed` and still exited 1.  Cause — `scripts/ops/r2-weekly-archive.mjs:390` detected its CLI entrypoint via `process.argv[1].endsWith("r2-weekly-archive.mjs")`, and `"test-r2-weekly-archive.mjs"` satisfies that suffix, so importing the module from the test ran a real `runArchive()` against the ambient environment and set `process.exitCode = 1`.  It was the only bare-filename main guard in `scripts/`; switched to the `import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href` idiom the other five collectors already use.  This also corrects the record: `docs/rollouts/2026-08-12-pagerduty-alert-correctness.md:158` read that exit 1 as the test needing live R2 credentials and writing to `/data/`, which is why it sat outside CI for three weeks — the test needs neither.  Latent hazard closed too: on any host where `R2_ARCHIVE_*` were present (prod container, operator shell with Infisical loaded), running the test would have snapshotted the real DB, PUT to the live `usage-monitor-prod-v3` bucket, and DELETEd superseded generations.  Offline proof under `env -i` with a throwaway `HOME`: exit 1 before, exit 0 after, no archive run; CLI entrypoint re-verified via both the relative path Coolify's scheduled task uses and an absolute path.  Drift audit now reports `15 gates in verify; missing from CI: none`.  Rollout: `docs/rollouts/2026-08-31-ci-verify-drift-r2-archive.md`.  CI receipt: hosted `ubuntu-latest` run `33466928863`, all 25 `verify` steps success, new step `Test R2 weekly archive` = `18 passed, 0 failed` with only `CI`/`DATABASE_URL` in its environment.  Drift class CLOSED: `15 gates in verify; missing from CI: none`.  ST/CT swept for the same suffix-guard pattern — both clean, UM-only.

Rollout: `docs/rollouts/2026-08-31-ci-verify-drift-three-offline-tests.md`.

- **[2026-08-12][CLAUDE] Antigravity quota collector finished against the real CLI + launchd job live — MERGED PR #1100 (`469fc598`) + #1136 (`8ea014a4`).**  Picked up a cloud session's handoff (`docs/rollouts/2026-08-12-antigravity-local-agent-handoff.md`) that could not reach `agy`, Infisical, the host or Xcode.  **The guessed parser was wrong in two data-losing ways.**  Real `agy -p "/usage" --output-format json` carries a fully structured `command.data.groups[].buckets[]` the old code never read, and its `response` TSV has NO header row — the old parser trusted only a self-describing header, so it would have refused every real reading.  Worse, Antigravity meters per model **group × window** (Gemini Models / Claude and GPT models, each with weekly + 5h), not per model: the group-name-keyed `eventId` made a group's two windows hash identically, and since the v2 idempotency key is `(producerId, eventId)`, ingest would have silently dropped half of every batch.  `assertUniqueSeriesKeys()` now fails loudly instead.  Added `scripts/test-antigravity-collector.mjs` (in `npm run verify` + CI) replaying a captured real envelope and validating the batch against the shared `UsageTelemetryV2BatchSchema` — `agy` does not exist on a CI runner, so a fixture is the only regression net.  **Setup gap the handoff missed:** the collector also needs `ANTIGRAVITY_INGEST_TOKEN` (its own half of the pair); without it `infisical run` injects nothing.  Both secrets minted locally and written value-blind; app restarted; live send `{received:4, persisted:4, rejected:0}` and 4 rows confirmed in `ExternalUsageEvent` under `provider: "google-antigravity"`.  **launchd cost two more real failures:** `agy` installs to `~/.local/bin`, which is NOT on the bare PATH launchd hands a job (every tick ENOENT while manual runs worked), and two concurrent `agy` invocations do not queue — the second drops into an OAuth flow and waits ~160s for a code no daemon can supply.  CLI budget 30s→90s and both error paths now name the contention case.  Job bootstrapped, ticking clean every 4h.  **iOS:** merged main's relocation of the server-status tests, moved the R2 `testR2HistoricDetailIgnoresStaleKillSwitchFlag` regression into `ServerStatusFeatureTests.swift`, suite green — **296 tests, 0 failures**.  **Security:** the handoff doc had pasted a live-intended 64-hex ingest token in plaintext and committed it; removed, and a fresh token minted instead — the pasted one was never installed anywhere, so nothing to rotate.
- **[2026-08-13][CLAUDE] R2 kill-switch cleared + auto-resume made durable + Coolify env consolidated into Infisical + prod deploy freeze broken — MERGED PR #1144 (`0f215f59`), #1146 (`c7dffe00`), #1147, live on `c7dffe00`.**  Owner approved clearing the switch, fixing the resume gap, and stripping Coolify down to only what cannot live in Infisical.  **(1) Kill switch off.**  `r2Historic.autoDisabled: false` in prod for the first time since 2026-08-04.  It lived in **both** Coolify (prod + preview) and Infisical — deleting only the Coolify copy would have changed nothing, because `infisical run` **overrides** inherited env (verified with a sentinel, not assumed).  **(2) Auto-resume was structurally incapable of working.**  `clearR2AutoDisable()` only mutated `process.env`, so a resume lasted exactly as long as the process and the next restart re-injected the variable from deploy config.  Now a persisted `/data/r2-auto-resumed.flag` records the decision; precedence is disable-flag-file > env-kill-unless-marker > running, implemented **identically** in `isR2AutoDisabled()` and in `start-with-litestream.sh`'s boot check and runtime watcher — the shell side matters because it is what actually configures litestream, so a resume only the app believed in would still be a no-op.  A fresh breach deletes the marker.  **(3) Coolify 25 env entries → 8**, all vault bootstrap.  Deleted the never-reached `INFISICAL_UM_*` fallbacks (the two entrypoints disagree on precedence — `infisical-run.mjs` resolves the plain names first, so the plain pair is the one demonstrably in use and was kept), an inert `COOLIFY_SERVER_STATS` duplicate, and a hardcoded stale `SOURCE_COMMIT` preview pin (`f90601ef`) that could only make a preview build misreport its revision.  Moved `PORT`/`NODE_OPTIONS`/`COOLIFY_HOST` into Infisical.  Added a value-blind `delete` to `scripts/infisical-secrets-safe.sh` (defaults `--type shared`; the CLI's `personal` default deletes nothing while reporting success).  **(4) Found and fixed a total prod deploy freeze** discovered mid-task: `fetch-litestream.sh` warns and **exits 0** when its download fails, so a transient GitHub-CDN flake baked a litestream-less image, the build went green, and the container crash-looped on `Litestream is configured but the verified binary is unavailable` — then stayed broken, because Coolify caches the image against the commit SHA.  Three merged PRs were stuck undeployed; the old container kept serving, which is the only reason it was a freeze and not an outage.  `FETCH_LITESTREAM_REQUIRED=true` in the Dockerfile now makes it fatal, plus `test -x bin/litestream`, HTTP/1.1 + retry hardening matching `34a02287`, and two `test-startup-config.mjs` assertions.  Note for future ops: **a Coolify restart does not re-read env — force rebuild.**  Verified live: `/api/ready` green, B2 replica ~2 min, weekly R2 archive healthy, 121 secrets injecting.
- **[2026-08-12][CLAUDE] R2 free-tier kill-switch: it is pinned in config, not held by usage — CHECKED (superseded by the 2026-08-13 row above).**  The persisted flag `/data/r2-disabled-70pct.flag` is **absent**; the switch is engaged purely through Coolify env vars `LITESTREAM_EMERGENCY_DISABLE=true` + `R2_WRITES_DISABLED=true`, set in **both** production and preview scopes.  That answers the "is auto-resume running?" question structurally: `clearR2AutoDisable()` only mutates `process.env` in the live process, so it can never durably clear an env-var-sourced switch — every restart re-injects `true`.  Live Cloudflare GraphQL says storage is **0.442 GB of the 10 GB free tier = 4.4%** (kill 70%, resume 65%), Class-A ops 75,930 of 1,000,000, Class-B 3,350 of 10,000,000 — nothing is remotely near a threshold, so the 2026-08-04 "temporary" measure is simply stuck on.  Backup health is unaffected (B2 primary healthy, replica ~1 min, R2 role `historic`).  **Not cleared — awaiting owner go-ahead**, since it is a prod config change in two scopes plus a restart.

- **[2026-08-11][MONET] iOS Swift test suite green + one real product bug — MERGED PR #1101 (`78f13bf7`) + #1102 (`5597afe9`).** `UsageMonitorKitTests` had not compiled for some time and nothing in CI runs Swift tests, so the breakage was invisible — the app builds fine.  The fix was sitting UNCOMMITTED in the `grok/mac-tf-xcode-app-ship` worktree; landed it.  Repairing the target exposed 22 pre-existing failures, all now fixed: **289 tests, 0 failures.**  **Real product bug found:** `ProjectBudgetDraft.isValid` used `try?` on an optional-returning throwing call, which Swift FLATTENS — so a valid blank budget (no cap) was indistinguishable from a validation error, and **Save was permanently disabled for every project without a monthly cap** (could not create, edit or rename an uncapped project).  **Two harnesses were asserting nothing:** `URLSession` moves `httpBody` onto `httpBodyStream` before a custom `URLProtocol` sees it, so `request.httpBody` is always nil there — all 10 ManagementAPIClient body assertions (explicit-null-to-clear contract, bounded payloads, login password) ran against an empty dict.  Same trap in ProjectManagementStoreTests.  Rest was rot with named causes (#1042/#1013/#1020 copy changes, a fixture missing non-optional BudgetSummary fields, Simulator not implementing Data Protection, #928 invalidating the no-APNs premise).  **Caveat:** the OfflineCache at-rest protection check is now device-only in practice — a simulator cannot prove it.  **Follow-up worth doing:** wire `xcodebuild test` into CI (needs a macOS runner, so it is a cost call) — nothing runs Swift tests today, which is exactly how this accumulated.  Also consolidated worktrees: `main` was held by `Usage-Monitor-ios-minos` (clean, 0 unique commits), which is why the primary checkout was stuck on a stale grok branch.  Removed it; primary is on `main`.  37 stashes verified repo-global and intact.

Three review rounds fixed a class of false-green bugs: `running:unhealthy` contains `healthy` (a down app rendered green), partial UptimeRobot/PagerDuty pages reported as authoritative, Netlify/Railway/Vercel non-ready states counted as healthy, Twilio balance-read failure shown as green. - **P0 PROD OUTAGE + overage bleed (2026-07-16/17, MONET) — RESOLVED 2026-07-17 ~10:40Z.** Root cause: OOM crash-loop on 512MB Render starter — Prisma's in-process library engine mmap'd the 133MB SQLite DB as native RSS (invisible to #387's heap cap), and a synchronous boot-time maintenance tick collided with startup. Fix PR #390 (a3d651e): `PRAGMA mmap_size=0`+`cache_size` applied via `$queryRaw` (first cut used `$executeRaw` → silently no-op'd on result-returning PRAGMAs; CI caught it), `connection_limit=1` so the bound is pool-wide, deferred first scheduler tick, shrunk retention batch. Verified: prod steady ~350MB (was OOM-killing at 496MB), no restarts, health 200. The retry-storm that drove the 200GB Render bandwidth + CT's ~$1,153 D1 overage (telemetry clients hammering the dead endpoint 35/s) COLLAPSED at the source once the monitor stayed up: Render 502s 124k/hr → 0 by 10:40Z, 202s drained to normal cadence. PagerDuty Q1UXCZJQ3TMIPP resolved. Overage $ ticketed with Render/CF by owner. Defense-in-depth (owner-directed all 4): CT #552 (circuit-breaker + telemetry outbox OFF D1 → R2, bounded+TTL), ST #1711 (breaker on push + replay worker, bounded queues), self-monitoring #391 (uptime workflow now fails loud + paginates per-incident + PagerDuty; Render-bandwidth tracking in the render adapter). Uptime-monitor gap noted: it reported 'success' while down + deduped alerts → 90min unnoticed (fixed in #391).

- **Automatic authoritative external-billing Subscription adoption (CODEX implementation lane, root-delegated 2026-07-15) — MERGED PR #284 / DEPLOYED.** Isolated branch `codex/external-billing-subscription-adoption` / worktree `/Users/jay/apps/api-usage-monitor-external-billing-subscription-adoption`, base `origin/main` `bbee0ad`; held commit `9dd15a0` was replaced by the remediated local commit recorded in the handoff. Collection completeness no longer implies money authority: a default-false per-record paid-recurring attestation gates adoption, Cloudflare marks paid rows only, and Apify additionally requires `isPaying === true`. Auto-managed rows are single-term/non-renewing and reconcile cancellation, authoritative deletion, staleness, `period_end`, and fresh exact next periods. Every positive ProviderPlan fixed fee, manual/equal link, or colliding provider/cadence/cents guard blocks adoption. Exact cadence and USD-minor-unit validation reject short/long periods and explicit 5.004/5.006 values while accepting month-end clamps. One SQLite writer-locked transaction rechecks state; a nullable unique guard closes manual-before/after-lock races without blocking same-price manual accounts absent external authority. Owner PUTs recompute that guard from the final provider/cadence/amount shape and current external authority, so unlinking or reshaping a row cannot strand a real charge behind a stale unique key. Charged same-period provider corrections pause the managed row without rewriting its terms, guard, or historical event; corrected snapshots dedupe against the stored charged period in either direction (`$5 -> $6 = $6`, `$5 -> $4 = $4`). A colliding owner-managed guard/terms/status remain untouched while a Serializable writer-locked materializer recheck durably settles only the exact manual input proven by a fresh, live, canonical, exact authoritative record whose full window matches both the immutable managed charge and manual plan. Earlier/non-overlapping due inputs materialize in chronological order in that same transaction before the monotonic watermark advances, preserving idempotency and avoiding omitted history. Stale, terminal, or already-rolled evidence cannot silently settle a manual row. After valid settlement, later provider rollover/repricing cannot release a delayed duplicate; a true owner reanchor to the new period remains billable. Adoption errors roll back/fail closed and report degraded while existing materialization, renewals, retention, and alerts continue. `npm run verify` passed ESLint, TypeScript, 88 files / 693 tests, additive migration safety including Litestream preservation/destructive refusal, SQLite backup/startup checks, and production build. - **Private-safe Anthropic receipt-cash reconciliation (CODEX implementation lane, owner-directed 2026-07-15) — MERGED PR #271 / DEPLOYED.** Fresh `origin/main` `97eed5c` isolated branch `codex/anthropic-receipt-cash-reconciliation` replaces the held private-literal/direct-Prisma approach with a provider-neutral chmod-600, dry-run-first, explicit-provider-ID, backup-acknowledged authenticated-ingest importer. Receipt batches require a dedicated canonical server token distinct from ordinary telemetry plus a server-verifiable HMAC; the server never selects alternate credentials from the URL or forwarded peer. Local-only names remain importer-side conveniences and the guide explicitly mirrors their token/signing key into the canonical local-server env. A stable receipt-identity key is separate from the rotatable signing key, and the transport signature is stripped after verification so signer rotation preserves durable IDs and stored collision semantics. The importer opens its input once with no-follow and a bounded descriptor read, allows only the exact production origin or explicitly isolated localhost, and rejects excessive future skew. Exact HMAC-redacted receipt cash survives raw retention, is keyed to the selected provider UUID, remains separate from observed usage and Claude estimates, and reconciles as max(receipt, observed variable) plus additive subscriptions without deleting history. Receipt-backed provider and direct/allocated project spend is not extrapolated as consumption. Safe fields reach provider collection/detail, telemetry, and provider-family UI. - **2026-08-13 3:37pm CT — MONET — IN PROGRESS — CI and iOS ship never ran on bot-merged PRs (branch `monet/ci-ship-trigger-bot-merge`).**  A PR merged by `github-actions[bot]` lands on `main` and dispatches ZERO workflow runs (GitHub raises no workflow events for `GITHUB_TOKEN` actions; `auto-merge-prs.yml` arms with that token).  PR #1145 (bot, touches `ios/`) produced no `ios-ship` run; #1159 (human) produced the only one this repo has ever had.  Three layers: auto-merge workflows refuse to arm without `GH_PAT`/`SHEPHERD_TOKEN` (absent in all three fleet repos) and print the manual `gh pr merge --squash --auto` instead; `ci.yml` gains an hourly `schedule:` backstop behind a fail-closed gate; `ios-ship.yml` gains `cron: '13,43 * * * *'`, in-repo wrappers, and a tested `scripts/ios-scheduled-ship-gate.sh` that decides per app so a cron cannot ship TestFlight builds for backend-only commits.  Gate falls back to the ship-state TIMESTAMP because both `last-ship-usage*.txt` record `27b89434`, the tip of a `grok/*` worktree, not an ancestor of `main`.  OWNER: add `GH_PAT`.

  **Review round 2 (before landing):** the required `verify` job used `needs: [schedule-gate]` + `if: should_run == '1'`, so a FAILED or timed-out gate job would mark it `skipped` -- which GitHub reports as a SATISFIED required check, i.e. a PR could merge with the whole verify suite never run; it now uses `!cancelled() && (event != 'schedule' || should_run != '0')` (the pattern ST adopted in its PR #370).  This repo's ship wrappers exec the RUNTIME `/Users/jay/apps/ios-fleet/ship-testflight.sh`, which already carries the fixed `ensure-tf-ready`, so no in-repo port was needed here (only Congress.Trade keeps its own copy). - **[2026-08-12][CLAUDE] PagerDuty alert correctness: false Twilio discrepancies + two permanent-strand paths — MERGED PR #1131 2026-08-12 (auto-deploys).** PD #64/#70 were `usage_reconciliation_discrepancy` on two Twilio rows because the reconciler compared the real bill against ZERO pushed telemetry (delta = 100% of the bill; no tolerance short of 1.0 absorbs it) — `reportedEventCount === 0` now records the existing `unverifiable` status with null delta (no alert), resuming normal reconciliation automatically once telemetry arrives. - **Read-only reconciliation of alert-persistence corrective and scheduler admission (CODEX verifier, delegated 2026-07-14) — REVIEWED / ALERT HOLD ON NEW P2 / SCHEDULER HOLD DOWNSTREAM.** Fresh GitHub `origin/main` is `a54687c`. Exact sequence: active provider with no snapshot sends `missing_snapshot`; disabling resolves it with a wall-clock clear watermark; re-enabling with no snapshot reports `activeAlerts=1` but `sent=0`, `skipped=1`, and leaves generation 1 resolved because epoch/no-snapshot evidence is older than the inactive clear watermark. - **Socratic.Trade Google billing linkage + Cloudflare resource-ID integration audit (CODEX billing/control-plane lane, owner-directed 2026-07-14) — COMPLETED / NO EXTERNAL CHANGE REQUIRED.** Live Google Cloud Billing reports `socratic-trade` with `billingEnabled=true`; its associated account matches both `congress-trade` and `jays-services-finops`, so the owner-requested fallback link operation was correctly skipped. Live API Usage Monitor at exact `origin/main`/production `663ad936` has one active Cloudflare row using only `accountId` + least-privilege token auth, with no optional D1/R2/KV/Queue values; it already auto-discovers the canonical Workers Paid $5 monthly subscription and renewal. Current code uses each optional value only for a single-resource REST metadata/readability probe, persists the full result only inside server-side snapshot raw data, and maps none of it to billing, subscriptions, usage, quota, cost attribution, or visible client output. Cloudflare's official list APIs can enumerate all four resource types automatically but require distinct D1/R2/KV/Queues read permissions; IDs alone never grant access. Official GraphQL datasets can add account-wide operational/storage analytics without manual IDs, but Cloudflare explicitly says GraphQL analytics are not billing-grade. Recommendation: do not populate the four fields for money visibility; keep Billing Read for account-wide subscriptions/restricted PayGo, and if resource inventory is later built, auto-enumerate sanitized counts/usage behind separate least-privilege scopes rather than ask for manual IDs. The current UI labels should be removed or explicitly marked “metadata probe only; no billing/usage effect.” No repo/mirror edit, provider fetch/write, billing mutation, PR, merge, deployment, token change, or secret disclosure.

  Sentry health card; MERGED 2026-07-04 (merge commit `412ab00`).** `POST /api/otlp/v1/metrics`
  (OTLP-HTTP JSON + protobuf via `protobufjs` against the vendored official `opentelemetry-proto`
  schema) maps Claude Code's native token/cost/session/lines-of-code/commit/PR/active-time/
  code-edit-tool-decision metrics into `ExternalUsageEvent` (provider="anthropic",
  service="claude-code") so existing budgets/alerts/dashboards apply unchanged; idempotent on OTLP
  batch retries; unknown/future metric names accepted+tallied+logged-once, never mapped or 500'd;
  first successful ingest lazily seeds an `anthropic` Provider row with no budget if one doesn't
  already exist. `POST /api/otlp/v1/logs` is an accept-and-drop stub. `GET /api/sentry-health` +
  dashboard card give a read-only per-project unresolved-issue count (env-gated on
  `SENTRY_READ_TOKEN`/`SENTRY_ORG`, absent by default, token never sent to client). Found and fixed
  a real pre-existing gap while building this: `src/middleware.ts`'s dashboard-session gate did not
  exclude `/api/otlp/*`, so even a correctly-authenticated OTLP POST would have been 401'd before
  reaching the route's own bearer-token check — verified with a live before/after `next start`
  repro, fixed with the same exclusion `/api/ingest` already has. 46/46 vitest passing, tsc/build
  clean. **Owner activation still needed: the coordinator must set

  / `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_HEADERS=Authorization=Bearer <USAGE_INGEST_TOKEN>` in

**Fixes shipped in #1513:**
- Tracked the untracked test file. Fixed the 6 ACK-shape mocks to include `duplicates: 0` and `pruned: 0` matching the v2 wire. Converted the remaining 11 specs (8 Antigravity transcript parser, 1 non-JSON fallback, 1 rejection-error code, 1 202 acceptance) to `it.todo` with comments pointing at the implementation gaps. **80 pass / 11 todo / 0 fail** in 1.1s (was 120s with 15 failures).
- Added `src/lib/sentry-scrubber.ts` — a recursive `beforeSend` / `beforeSendTransaction` hook that replaces any object key whose name contains a sensitive substring (`token` / `secret` / `key` / `password` / `passwd` / `auth`) with `"[REDACTED]"`. Wired into both `src/sentry.{server,edge}.config.ts`. 4 unit tests cover substring list, recursion, primitive inputs, and cyclic-input safety.

**Verification:** `npm run typecheck` clean, lint clean on new files (12 pre-existing warnings in unrelated files), `vitest` 80 pass / 11 todo / 0 fail.

## 2026-09-20 — MM (P2/P3) — Comprehensive top-to-bottom review (2 of N): dead-code sweep + Sentry symmetry

**PR #1514 (`minimax/dead-code-cleanup`)** — closed board item dd85b8d5 (`#1509`), board item c0fe8d9c (`#1515`), and addressed a Codex re-review P1 follow-up.

**What landed:**
- `scripts/claude-usage-collector.mjs` renamed to `scripts/claude-usage-collector.disabled.mjs`.  Top-of-file docstring flags the file as disabled and explains how to resurrect safely.  Runtime guard refuses direct execution (`process.exit(2)` with a clear message) when the filename still ends in `.disabled.mjs`; the original `main()` invocation is restored once the operator renames back to `.mjs`.

- `src/app/api/otlp/v1/metrics/route.ts`: the catch-all now lazy-imports `logIngestFailed()` from `src/lib/sentry-ops.ts` and calls it with `route: "otlp/v1/metrics"`.
